description: Welcome to Lillian's Pan Pizza, located in Perdido Key in Pensacola, Florida. Known as a favorite eating-place for locals and tourists alike, Lillian's is one of the best pizza restaurants you'll try! We encourage you to bring the entire family for exceptional dining while enjoying the view and the atmosphere of beautiful Perdido Key beach. Chef Isnar personally hand makes our pizza dough every morning, and any left over dough is thrown out. The recipe is secret, and anyone who has eaten a pizza at Lillian's will testify to its quality and taste. This is probably why Lillian's has been featured in several well-known magazines, such as Pizza Today Magazine, 2002 and 2004, featured in "The Hot 100 Independents." Additional awards include Award of Culinary Excellence, 1998 in "Taste of the Times," and Best Seafood Pizza, 2002. As Lillian says, "While our pizzas are known to be excellent, we also have pasta, salads and sandwiches that will please everyone from the gourmet to the vegetarian." Visit Lillian's Pan Pizza today! Our team of professionals make all of our dough, meatballs and lasagna by hand using the freshest ingredients every single day. We are not only known for pizza, calzones and strombolis, our other offerings include specialty pastas, signature salads, sandwiches and wings. Don't forget our homemade Ranch dressing! We can accommodate any party, large or small, in our spacious indoor or outdoor seating areas.

--- entity_google_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
review_id: 5506
entity_slug: lillians-pizza
google_review_name: places/ChIJX-UjrECvkIgRIWzBc4_nZGI/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT2tGcGJHVXdOWGxvUkZKYWJWSTJVSEI0YWtKa2FWRRAB
rating: 2
review_text: Had very high expectations for this place after all the fan fair surrounding the place. Well the reality did not live up to the hype. Had planned on bringing a group here next week and thought we would try it out first. Arrived just after opening and was seated promptly. Ordered the house made meatballs as an appetizer. Found them very very spicy and very dense. They also had hard inedible chunks of meat on the outside. My wife ordered the club sandwich which came with mayo on the side. I've never heard of that but when in Rome. Problem didn't come with any mayo at all. Asked our waitress Shyrel for mayo, she couldn't find any. 10 minutes later after my wife ate 1/2 the sandwich dry we got 3 mayo packets. Shyrel said they moved it. The bacon on the club sandwich was so small and broken up im pretty sure it was bacon bits. Who serves a completely dry club sandwich with bacon bits? I ordered a 14" all meat pan pizza which was loaded with toppings, but the crust that was so raved about was me at best. I found it dry and greasy at the same time. I don't even know how you do that. Then to top it all off Shyrel kindly topped of my sprite with water. Thanks for that, but hey she gave us the senior discount! I really don't think this visit could have been worse. Obviously we will not be bringing our group here, and in all honestity we threw away the 3/4 pizza and meatballs we brought home because we could stomach eating it.

author_name: H. Hays

rating: 3
review_text: Food was alright. Prices were pretty steep for what we ordered. Staff was friendly and quick. I had the meatball sub and this is only my opinion, but the meatballs were way too big for the sandwich. I had to cut it up which would've been fine but the plate I was given was a basket with tissue paper on the bottom. So needless to say, before every bite I had to be sure there was no paper. The pizzas were good. Pepperoni and cheese. Wings were decent. Kids icees were 20 bucks just for a souvenir cup. Our bill for four was close to $200.00 before tip. Not sure what other pizza places charge around here but we won't be going back to this one.

id: 8e4fc2f8-d7ae-4e71-bede-afba984130c8
entity_slug: lillians-pizza
reviewer_name: H. Hays
rating: 2
body: Had very high expectations for this place after all the fan fair surrounding the place. Well the reality did not live up to the hype. Had planned on bringing a group here next week and thought we would try it out first. Arrived just after opening and was seated promptly. Ordered the house made meatballs as an appetizer. Found them very very spicy and very dense. They also had hard inedible chunks of meat on the outside. My wife ordered the club sandwich which came with mayo on the side. I've never heard of that but when in Rome. Problem didn't come with any mayo at all. Asked our waitress Shyrel for mayo, she couldn't find any. 10 minutes later after my wife ate 1/2 the sandwich dry we got 3 mayo packets. Shyrel said they moved it. The bacon on the club sandwich was so small and broken up im pretty sure it was bacon bits. Who serves a completely dry club sandwich with bacon bits? I ordered a 14" all meat pan pizza which was loaded with toppings, but the crust that was so raved about was me at best. I found it dry and greasy at the same time. I don't even know how you do that. Then to top it all off Shyrel kindly topped of my sprite with water. Thanks for that, but hey she gave us the senior discount! I really don't think this visit could have been worse. Obviously we will not be bringing our group here, and in all honestity we threw away the 3/4 pizza and meatballs we brought home because we could stomach eating it.

The pizzas were good. Pepperoni and cheese. Wings were decent. Kids icees were 20 bucks just for a souvenir cup. Our bill for four was close to $200.00 before tip. Not sure what other pizza places charge around here but we won't be going back to this one.
